FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models
Authors: Yalun Wu, Junfeng Fang, Jiawei Wang, Haotian Liu, Qijun Yang, Minghan Yang, Hongcheng Guo, Zhoujun Li, Boyang Wang
Venue: Proceedings of the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM), 2026
First: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLY-EVAL++：一种基于证据的安全约束飞行预测评估协议
在安全关键、物理约束的环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）需要的不仅仅是基于准确性的指标，因为数值上接近真实值的预测仍然可能违反操作约束、以物理不一致的方式组合领域，或未能生成可用的结构化输出。现有的评估协议无法可靠地测量这些失败模式。我们提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据的评估协议，结合了对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全约束的确定性验证，以及通过固定评分标准引导的聚合，形成可解释的多维评分。我们通过扩展PilotBench设置，结合历史条件和多步预测任务，为飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）实例化FLY-EVAL++。在66个LLM中，安全合规性是模型行为中最具区分性的维度：具有可比预测性能的模型在安全评分上相差超过28分，我们观察到反复出现的失败，包括在物理上合理的预测下的安全违规和多步展开中的不稳定性。这些结果表明，在安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足和结构有效性，而不是仅依赖于以准确性为中心的报告。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for effective evaluation protocols for large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical environments, where traditional accuracy metrics are insufficient due to potential violations of operational constraints and physical inconsistencies. Previous methods fail to reliably assess these critical failure modes, prompting the development of FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that integrates deterministic verification of compliance with safety constraints and physical feasibility, along with a structured scoring system. This approach is well-motivated as it aims to enhance the reliability of LLM evaluations in contexts where safety is paramount. The methodology involves applying FLY-EVAL++ to Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) tasks, revealing significant discrepancies in safety compliance among models with similar predictive performance. The findings indicate that safety compliance is a crucial dimension of model behavior, highlighting the necessity for evaluations to explicitly measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity rather than relying solely on accuracy metrics.
本研究关注于在安全关键环境中有效评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的需求，传统的基于准确性的指标无法捕捉操作约束和物理一致性，因此显得不足。以往的方法未能可靠地测量这些失败模式，导致对模型在实际应用中表现的理解存在差距。提出的FLY-EVAL++协议引入了一个综合框架，结合了对安全约束和物理可行性的确定性验证，以及结构化评分系统。该方法应用于飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP），揭示了66个LLMs在安全合规性方面的显著差异，尽管预测性能相似，但安全评分相差超过28分。这些发现强调了在安全关键领域评估约束满足和结构有效性的重要性，超越了单纯的准确性指标。
IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks
Authors: Saikat Mondal, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu, Asif Ekbal
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 33 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Contains examples of harmful model outputs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IndicSafeEval：多语言说服性越狱攻击下大型语言模型的安全鲁棒性
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多语言环境中的使用日益增加，但其安全性仍主要在英语中进行评估。这限制了我们对低资源和文化多样语言中对齐失败表现的理解。我们引入了IndicSafeEval，这是一个针对印度语言的基于说服的越狱评估框架。我们的基准结合了十个安全关键内容类别和六种类人类的说服策略，涵盖四种不同的印度语言，如印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语，共生成7200个对抗性提示。我们对多个开源LLM进行了系统的黑箱评估，以检查它们的安全行为在不同语言、说服策略和风险类别中的变化。我们的分析表明，模型在所有语言和提示风格中并不表现出相同的安全性。相反，安全性能在很大程度上依赖于所使用的语言和请求的措辞方式。我们进一步观察到，不同的风险类别表现出不同的脆弱性，一些类型的有害内容对基于说服的越狱攻击显著更敏感。这些发现揭示了当前安全评估的重要局限性，主要集中在英语，并强调了需要多语言和说服意识的基准框架，以更准确地评估现实世界中的LLM安全性。我们的实现可在https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval获取。警告：本文包含可能冒犯或有害的示例数据。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current safety evaluations of large language models (LLMs), which predominantly focus on English, thereby neglecting multilingual contexts and the unique challenges posed by low-resource languages. Previous methods have not adequately assessed how alignment failures manifest in diverse linguistic and cultural settings, leading to a gap in understanding safety robustness across languages. The proposed IndicSafeEval framework introduces a novel evaluation approach that combines various safety-critical content categories with persuasive strategies in multiple Indian languages, resulting in a comprehensive dataset of 7,200 adversarial prompts. This methodology allows for a systematic black-box evaluation of LLMs, revealing significant variations in safety performance based on language and phrasing. The findings indicate that certain risk categories are more vulnerable to persuasion-based attacks, highlighting the necessity for multilingual and persuasion-aware safety assessments, thus contributing to a more nuanced understanding of LLM safety in diverse contexts.

TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-01T03:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：用于LLM越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选项来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选项往往在搜索后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明候选选择，而不仅仅是候选生成，是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选项的即时损失来选择候选项，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which typically select candidates based solely on immediate loss, leading to suboptimal long-term outcomes due to a phenomenon known as selection-stage reward hacking. The proposed method, TACS, introduces a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework that enhances the evaluation process by incorporating a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizing selection through reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction. This approach effectively mitigates the issues associated with myopic candidate selection, demonstrating a significant contribution to the field. Experimental results on HarmBench indicate that TACS outperforms strong baseline methods, achieving higher attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior within the same search budget, thus supporting the goal of improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
本研究解决了现有基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常专注于即时损失最小化，导致由于选择阶段的奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。提出的方法TACS引入了一种轨迹感知的候选选择框架，通过结合轨迹感知代理增强评估过程，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择。这种方法有效缓解了与短视候选选择相关的问题，从而提高了攻击成功率并实现了更稳定的优化行为。在HarmBench上进行的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算内始终优于强基线方法，验证了其在增强越狱后缀优化方面的有效性。
Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Qilong Wu, Sahil Wadhwa, Pranab Mohanty, Giri Iyengar, Varun Chandrasekaran
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00
Comments: To appear on EMNLP 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对大型语言模型的有效性感知越狱评估
越狱的鲁棒性已成为大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的核心，但现有方法主要依赖于拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配启发式，这些方法强调语言的合理性而非正确性。我们识别出现有评估的一个关键限制：许多越狱意图依赖于指令有效性而非认知事实性，使得看似合理的响应被标记为成功，尽管在事实或程序上是错误的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了顺序认知和行动级验证（SEAV），这是一个以验证为中心的越狱评估框架，它将响应分解为有序步骤，并评估有效性和正确性。SEAV结合了LLM作为评判机制的语义解释与基于检索的外部知识源验证，评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上连贯，并在操作上能够推进有害目标。实证结果表明，SEAV将SD-A（一个策划的战略不诚实诊断）的假阳性率降低了14.9个百分点，相较于最强基线，并在四个公共基准中的三个重新将22.1%至51.0%的先前标记的成功重新分类为无效。这些结果表明，强制正确性显著重塑了测量的鲁棒性：许多先前标记的越狱成功被重新分类为无效，且结果在测试的搜索后端和评估模型中保持稳定。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical issue of jailbreak robustness in large language models (LLMs), highlighting limitations in existing evaluation methods that focus on linguistic plausibility rather than factual correctness. Previous methodologies primarily relied on refusal behavior and intent-matching heuristics, which often misclassify realistic-looking but factually incorrect responses as successful. The proposed Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV) framework offers a verification-centric approach that decomposes responses into ordered steps, assessing both validity and correctness by integrating LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms with retrieval-grounded verification. This novel methodology significantly reduces the false-positive rate on the SD-A diagnostic by 14.9 percentage points compared to the strongest baseline and reclassifies a substantial portion of previously labeled successes as invalid across multiple benchmarks. These findings demonstrate that prioritizing correctness can fundamentally alter the evaluation of jailbreak robustness in LLMs.

Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services
Authors: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
First: 2026-03-11T14:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T14:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM safety evaluations rely on binary attack-success rates and domain-agnostic taxonomies, leaving regulated Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) deployments exposed to failures elicited through legally or professionally plausible framing. We introduce RAHS (Risk-Adjusted Harm Score), a risk-sensitive metric jointly capturing disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, and FinRedTeamBench, a 989-prompt benchmark spanning seven BFSI risk areas and 34 sub-categories mapped to regulatory frameworks. Evaluation uses an ensemble of three heterogeneous LLM judges, validated against human experts, and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline. On nine open-weight models, RAHS preserves separation under near-ceiling ASR, ranking is stable under hyperparameter sweeps, and multi-turn pressure drives not only more jailbreaks but more operationally severe disclosures, exposing failure modes that single-turn, domain-agnostic evaluations cannot reveal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对金融服务中大型语言模型的自动红队风险调整伤害评分
现有的大型语言模型安全评估依赖于二元攻击成功率和领域无关的分类法，使受监管的银行、金融服务和保险（BFSI）部署面临通过合法或专业合理框架引发的失败风险。我们引入了RAHS（风险调整伤害评分），这是一种风险敏感的指标，联合捕捉披露严重性、免责声明缓解和评审者间一致性，以及FinRedTeamBench，这是一个涵盖七个BFSI风险领域和34个子类别的989个提示基准，映射到监管框架。评估使用三位异构大型语言模型评审者的集成，经过与人类专家的验证，以及自适应多轮红队流程。在九个开放权重模型上，RAHS在接近上限的攻击成功率下保持分离，排名在超参数搜索中稳定，多轮压力不仅驱动更多越狱行为，还导致更严重的操作性披露，暴露出单轮、领域无关评估无法揭示的失败模式。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the inadequacies of existing safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs) in the Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) sectors, which often rely on binary attack-success rates and do not consider the specific regulatory context. Previous methods have failed to capture the nuanced risks associated with these domains, leading to potential vulnerabilities. The proposed Risk-Adjusted Harm Score (RAHS) offers a more comprehensive metric that evaluates disclosure severity and inter-judge agreement while being tailored to BFSI regulations. The methodology involves an ensemble of three diverse LLM judges and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline, validated against human experts. The results demonstrate that RAHS maintains effective differentiation among models under high attack success rates and reveals critical failure modes that traditional evaluations overlook, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM safety in regulated environments.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）在银行、金融服务和保险（BFSI）领域安全评估的局限性，这些评估通常依赖于二元攻击成功率和通用分类法，使这些系统容易受到微妙失败的影响。提出的风险调整危害评分（RAHS）提供了一种风险敏感的指标，评估披露严重性、免责声明缓解和评审者间一致性，同时引入了FinRedTeamBench，这是一个涵盖多个BFSI风险领域的989个提示的综合基准。该方法通过利用三种不同的LLM评审者的集成，并与人类专家进行验证，以及自适应的多轮红队测试流程，改善了过去的方法，有效捕捉到单轮评估无法发现的操作性严重披露。该方法论表明，RAHS在超参数变化下保持性能稳定，并揭示了九个开放权重模型中的关键失败模式，从而支持了在受监管环境中增强LLM安全性的目标。
Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-15T14:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T11:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating data remains a costly bottleneck for supervised NLP. Active learning (AL) reduces the number of human labels needed by selecting only the most informative instances, while instruction-tuned LLMs attack the same bottleneck from the other side, making labels cheap enough to annotate entire corpora. This raises two questions: can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be cheaply labeled? We investigate both by training supervised hate speech classifiers on a new dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, comparing human and LLM annotation under matched conditions. LLM annotation at scale outperforms human-supervised classifiers at roughly one-tenth the cost, for both a closed-source (GPT-5.2) and an open-weight (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) LLM, and the advantage is robust under soft-label evaluation. It hinges on the annotation interface: only a two-question decomposition mirroring the human annotation task unlocks it. AL provides no reliable advantage over random sampling in our prefiltered pool. Error structure depends on the LLM: only GPT-5.2 matches the human FP/FN balance, while other variants over-flag border-control and economic-competition discourse. Humans remain essential as evaluators; for training labels, the question shifts to which LLM, which interface, and what shape of pool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们还需要人类参与吗？在TikTok仇恨言论检测中人类与LLM注释的主动学习比较
数据注释仍然是监督NLP的一个成本瓶颈。主动学习（AL）通过仅选择最具信息量的实例来减少所需的人类标签数量，而经过指令调优的LLM则从另一个角度解决同一瓶颈，使标签变得足够便宜以注释整个语料库。这引发了两个问题：LLM标签能否替代AL循环中的人类标签，以及当整个语料库可以被廉价标注时，AL是否仍然必要？我们通过在一个包含278K条德国政治TikTok评论的新数据集上训练监督仇恨言论分类器来研究这两个问题，比较在匹配条件下的人类和LLM注释。大规模的LLM注释在成本约为人类监督分类器的十分之一的情况下表现优于人类监督分类器，无论是闭源（GPT-5.2）还是开放权重（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）LLM，并且在软标签评估下这一优势是稳健的。这取决于注释接口：只有与人类注释任务相对应的两个问题的分解才能解锁它。在我们预过滤的池中，AL对随机抽样没有可靠的优势。错误结构依赖于LLM：只有GPT-5.2匹配人类的FP/FN平衡，而其他变体则过度标记边境控制和经济竞争话语。人类作为评估者仍然是必不可少的；对于训练标签，问题转向哪个LLM、哪个接口以及什么样的池。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of data annotation in supervised natural language processing (NLP), particularly in the context of hate speech detection on TikTok, where traditional methods are costly and time-consuming. Previous approaches relied heavily on human annotation, which is expensive and limited in scalability, while recent advancements in instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) have made it possible to generate labels at a lower cost. This study proposes to evaluate whether LLM-generated labels can effectively replace human annotations in active learning (AL) scenarios and whether AL is still necessary when LLMs can label entire datasets affordably. The methodology involves training hate speech classifiers on a dataset of 278,000 German political TikTok comments, comparing the performance of classifiers trained on human versus LLM annotations. The findings reveal that LLM annotation significantly outperforms human annotation in terms of cost and efficiency, with the right annotation interface being crucial for success. However, AL does not provide a reliable advantage over random sampling, indicating that while LLMs can replace human labels for training, human evaluators remain essential for assessing model performance.
本研究解决了监督自然语言处理（NLP）中数据标注的挑战，特别是在TikTok仇恨言论检测的背景下，传统方法过于依赖人工输入，导致过程成本高且耗时。以往的方法，包括主动学习（AL），旨在通过选择信息量大的实例来减少人工标注，但未能充分利用能够以更低成本生成标签的指令调优大型语言模型（LLMs）的潜力。本文提出了一种将LLMs整合到标注过程中的方法，表明LLM生成的标签可以有效替代主动学习场景中的人工标签。研究涉及在一个包含278,000条德国政治TikTok评论的数据集上训练仇恨言论分类器，结果显示LLM标注在成本约为人工标注的十分之一的情况下表现更佳，尽管AL在预筛选池中并未提供显著的优势。研究结果表明，尽管LLMs可以提高标注效率，但人工评估者在评估标注质量方面仍然至关重要，这促使我们重新审视LLMs和人工在标注过程中的角色。
From Detection to Refusal: Safer LLMs via Circuit-Guided Weight Scaling
Authors: Kuan-Lin Chu, Chung-En Sun, Tsui-Wei Weng
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T08:01:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of the 2026 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing (EMNLP 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从检测到拒绝：通过电路引导的权重缩放实现更安全的LLM
尽管进行了广泛的对齐努力，大型语言模型（LLM）在对抗性提示下仍然容易生成不安全内容，但实现安全行为的内部机制仍然不够清楚。我们从机制可解释性的角度研究LLM安全性，并描述一个多阶段的*安全电路*，该电路组织拒绝行为，包括（i）响应有害输入的$\textbf{有害检测头}$，（ii）在残差流中介导和稳定安全信号的$\textbf{安全神经元}$，以及（iii）将这些信号转化为安全响应生成的$\textbf{拒绝头}$。通过针对性注意头和神经元级干预，我们提供了与该电路组织一致的因果证据，表明抑制上游有害检测头会干扰下游拒绝行为，而安全神经元介导了这种互动。我们验证了这种分解在多个LLM架构和对抗攻击设置中反复出现，并使用简单的、保持架构的权重缩放作为机制探针来测试其功能相关性。在六个LLM中，电路引导的缩放在攻击下提高了26.5%的安全率，同时在四个标准基准上仅造成1.7%的准确率下降。总体而言，我们的结果支持LLM安全性的电路级解释，并表明机制抽象可以揭示对齐行为背后的稳定和可转移模式。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the ongoing vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generating unsafe content despite alignment efforts, highlighting the need for a better understanding of the internal mechanisms that govern safety behaviors. Previous methods have primarily focused on alignment without adequately addressing the mechanistic aspects of safety, leading to insufficient robustness against adversarial prompts. The proposed approach introduces a multi-stage safety circuit comprising Harmful Detection Heads, Safety Neurons, and Refusal Heads, which collectively enhance the model's ability to refuse unsafe prompts. The methodology involves targeted interventions at the attention-head and neuron levels, providing causal evidence for the circuit's organization and validating its relevance across various LLM architectures. The results demonstrate a 26.5% improvement in safety rates under adversarial attacks with only a 1.7% accuracy drop on standard benchmarks, supporting the effectiveness of the circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and its potential for broader application.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在对抗性提示下生成不安全内容的持续脆弱性，尽管进行了对齐努力，但对安全行为的内部机制缺乏理解。以往的方法未能有效表征这些机制，导致在对抗条件下安全响应不足。提出的方法引入了一个多阶段安全电路，包括有害检测头、安全神经元和拒绝头，这些组件共同增强了对LLMs中拒绝行为的理解和实施。该方法论涉及在注意力头和神经元层面进行针对性干预，提供了电路组织的因果证据，并通过保持架构的权重缩放测试其功能相关性。结果显示，在六个LLMs中，攻击下的安全率提高了26.5%，而在标准基准测试中的准确率仅下降了1.7%，从而支持了LLM安全的电路级解释，并揭示了对齐行为中的稳定模式。
Turning the Spell Around: Lightweight Alignment Amplification via Rank-One Safety Injection
Authors: Harethah Abu Shairah, Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud, George Turkiyyah, Bernard Ghanem
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2025-08-28T13:22:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T07:43:32+00:00
Comments: EMNLP 2026, Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) often involves mediating internal representations to refuse harmful requests. Recent research has demonstrated that these safety mechanisms can be bypassed by ablating or removing specific representational directions within the model. In this paper, we propose the opposite approach: Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI), a white-box method that amplifies a model's safety alignment by permanently steering its activations toward the refusal-mediating subspace. ROSI operates as a simple, fine-tuning-free rank-one weight modification applied to all residual stream write matrices. The required safety direction can be computed from a small set of harmful and harmless instruction pairs. We show that ROSI consistently increases safety refusal rates - as evaluated by Llama Guard 3 - while preserving the utility of the model on standard benchmarks such as MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. Furthermore, we show that ROSI can also re-align 'uncensored' models by amplifying their own latent safety directions, demonstrating its utility as an effective last-mile safety procedure. Our results suggest that targeted, interpretable weight steering is a cheap and potent mechanism to improve LLM safety, complementing more resource-intensive fine-tuning paradigms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扭转咒语：通过一阶安全注入实现轻量级对齐放大
大型语言模型（LLMs）中的安全对齐通常涉及调解内部表示以拒绝有害请求。最近的研究表明，这些安全机制可以通过消除或移除模型内特定的表示方向来绕过。在本文中，我们提出了相反的方法：一阶安全注入（ROSI），这是一种白盒方法，通过永久性地将模型的激活引导到拒绝调解子空间来放大模型的安全对齐。ROSI作为一种简单的、无需微调的一阶权重修改，应用于所有残差流写矩阵。所需的安全方向可以从一小组有害和无害的指令对中计算得出。我们展示了ROSI在评估Llama Guard 3时始终提高安全拒绝率，同时在MMLU、HellaSwag和Arc等标准基准上保持模型的效用。此外，我们还展示了ROSI可以通过放大自身潜在安全方向来重新对齐“未审查”模型，证明其作为有效的最后一公里安全程序的实用性。我们的结果表明，针对性的、可解释的权重引导是一种廉价而有效的机制，可以提高LLM的安全性，补充更资源密集的微调范式。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs), where existing methods often fail due to the ability of harmful requests to bypass safety mechanisms by altering specific internal representations. The proposed approach, Rank-One Safety Injection (ROSI), differs from past methods by amplifying safety alignment instead of attempting to mediate harmful requests, utilizing a straightforward rank-one weight modification applied to residual stream write matrices without requiring fine-tuning. This method is well-motivated as it allows for the computation of safety directions from a limited set of instruction pairs, leading to a consistent increase in safety refusal rates as evaluated by Llama Guard 3, while maintaining model utility on standard benchmarks like MMLU, HellaSwag, and Arc. The contribution of this paper lies in demonstrating that targeted weight steering can effectively enhance LLM safety in a cost-efficient manner, serving as a viable last-mile safety procedure for both standard and 'uncensored' models.

Single Canonical Prompts Underestimate LLM Safety's Surface-Form Sensitivity
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Junwei Yao, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Wenbo Ye, Jiaxi Wen, Lai Yun Choi
First: 2026-08-01T22:28:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-30T05:09:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Sci-FM Workshop @ COLM 2026 (non-archival). Workshop version with reviews: https://openreview.net/forum?id=mZh0MqpOOC
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A benchmark score is a measurement instrument, yet most benchmarks read each item at a single canonical surface form. We ask whether that reading is faithful: when an item's intent is held fixed and only its meaning-preserving surface form varies, does the canonical-form score estimate model behavior well, and how much of any variation is decoding/judge noise rather than signal? We instantiate this in safety, a high-stakes setting with no gold label to average toward. To avoid prior confounds, we pre-author the reformulations (refusal-free, mostly non-LLM: machine back-translation and a Matrix-Language-Frame code-switch generator) so an identical surface form reaches every model, score all responses with one human-anchored, vendor-neutral judge (Claude, kappa = 0.86 vs. human on unsafe compliance, stable across languages, cross-checked by GPT-4o), and verify intent preservation. On 370 seeds x 5 surface forms x 5 models, no single transformation is uniformly most dangerous (6 of 20 per-transformation McNemar tests survive correction, most protective). Yet evaluating only the canonical prompt underestimates unsafe compliance: the union of unsafe outcomes across forms exceeds even the worst single form by 3.3-12.9 pp, with bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero for all five models, and 5-13% of seeds safe on canonical are unsafe under some reformulation -- above a zero stochasticity floor (canonical resampled five times at temperature 0 gives 0/370 new exposures). The size of this gap is model-dependent (largest on Gemini 2.5 Pro). One form recovers only ~53% of a model's observed unsafe surface and about three reach 85% -- a redundancy characterization of this form set, not of a defined population. A benign control (XSTest) suggests the instability is bidirectional, though the benign and harmful pools are not item-matched. We release the dataset, code, and per-response labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：单一规范提示低估了大型语言模型安全性的表面形式敏感性
基准分数是一种测量工具，但大多数基准以单一规范表面形式读取每个项目。我们询问这种读取是否忠实：当一个项目的意图保持不变，仅其保持意义的表面形式变化时，规范形式分数是否能很好地估计模型行为，以及任何变化中有多少是解码/评判噪声而非信号？我们在安全性这一高风险环境中实例化这一点，因其没有金标准可供平均。为了避免先前的混淆，我们预先授权了重构（无拒绝，主要非大型语言模型：机器回译和矩阵语言框架代码切换生成器），以便相同的表面形式到达每个模型，所有响应由一个人类锚定的、中立的评审（Claude，kappa = 0.86与人类在不安全合规性上的比较，跨语言稳定，由GPT-4o交叉验证），并验证意图保持。在370个种子x 5个表面形式x 5个模型中，没有单一变换是普遍最危险的（20个每变换的McNemar测试中有6个在校正后存活，大多数是保护性的）。然而，仅评估规范提示低估了不安全合规性：跨形式的不安全结果的并集甚至超过了最糟糕的单一形式3.3-12.9个百分点，所有五个模型的自助法95%置信区间均不包括零，且5-13%的在规范下安全的种子在某些重构下是不安全的——高于零随机性底线（规范在温度0下重采样五次给出0/370个新暴露）。这一差距的大小依赖于模型（在Gemini 2.5 Pro上最大）。一种形式仅恢复了模型观察到的不安全表面的约53%，而大约三种形式达到了85%——这是对这一形式集的冗余特征，而非定义人口的特征。一个良性对照（XSTest）表明不稳定性是双向的，尽管良性和有害池并未按项目匹配。我们发布了数据集、代码和每个响应标签。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing benchmark evaluations in assessing large language models (LLMs) by questioning the reliability of single canonical surface forms in estimating model behavior, particularly in safety contexts where no gold standard exists. Previous methods often overlooked the variability introduced by different surface forms, leading to potential misestimations of unsafe compliance. The proposed approach involves pre-authoring reformulations and using a consistent human-anchored judge to evaluate model responses across multiple surface forms, thus ensuring intent preservation and reducing confounding factors. The study finds that relying solely on canonical prompts significantly underestimates unsafe compliance, with variations across forms revealing that the combined unsafe outcomes exceed those of any single form. The methodology demonstrates that no single transformation is uniformly the most dangerous, and the results indicate a substantial gap in safety assessments, with implications for model evaluation and safety standards in LLMs.
本研究针对现有基准在评估大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性方面的局限性，传统方法通常依赖于单一的规范表面形式，这可能无法准确反映模型行为。作者提出了一种新方法，通过预先编写多个提示的改写，以确保意图一致，同时变化表面形式，从而减轻混淆因素。他们的方法论包括对370个种子和五个模型的全面评估，结果显示，仅依赖规范提示显著低估了不安全合规性，跨改写的危险结果总和超过最差单一形式3.3-12.9个百分点。研究结果表明，没有单一的改写形式是普遍最危险的，强调了安全评估的模型依赖性以及多样化提示评估捕捉模型行为全谱的必要性。
NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation
Authors: Zhiyuan Xu, Muhammad Firhard Roslan, Joseph Gardiner, Sana Belguith, Lichao Wu
First: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-26T12:44:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome. This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronFuzz：安全神经元引导的LLM安全评估模糊测试
安全评估对于评估对齐的大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能够抵御越狱攻击至关重要。然而，现有的自动化测试方法主要依赖于响应级反馈：每个候选提示通常需要生成目标模型的响应以评估其攻击有效性。这个过程成本高昂，更重要的是，对于大多数候选者在强对齐模型中被拒绝的情况下，仅提供稀疏的指导。 本文提出了NeuronFuzz，一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用内部安全神经元作为LLM安全评估的连续执行反馈。SafetyOracle将安全神经元激活转换为连续的安全警报分数，作为模糊测试的反馈，并且可以在预填充期间获得，从而消除模糊测试循环中的响应生成。为了构建SafetyOracle，NeuronFuzz使用模板不变的有害和良性输入以及稳定性感知选择，识别出一组紧凑的安全神经元，其激活捕捉有害意图识别。此外，由于安全警报分数是可微分的，NeuronFuzz利用其梯度识别安全敏感的模板位置，并使用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害负载，避免额外的优化变量。我们在21个文本和多模态模型上评估了NeuronFuzz。在五个白盒源模型中，它实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，超越基线最多48个百分点。其优化模板进一步零-shot转移到开放权重和六个专有目标模型，分别实现69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%的平均ASR和前5集成ASR（EASR）。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for effective safety evaluation of aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, highlighting the limitations of existing automated testing methods that rely on response-level feedback, which is costly and provides limited guidance. The proposed NeuronFuzz framework differs by utilizing internal safety neurons to provide continuous execution feedback, thus eliminating the need for generating model responses during fuzzing. This approach is well-motivated as it allows for more efficient identification of harmful intents through a SafetyOracle that converts neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score. The methodology involves using template-invariant inputs and stability-aware selection to pinpoint safety neurons, and leveraging differentiable safety scores to generate context-compatible mutations. NeuronFuzz demonstrates significant contributions by achieving a jailbreak discovery rate of 76-100% across five white-box source models, outperforming existing methods by up to 48 percentage points, and achieving high average attack success rates on various models.
本研究解决了对齐的大型语言模型（LLM）在抵御越狱攻击方面有效安全评估的迫切需求，强调了现有依赖响应级反馈的自动化测试方法的局限性，这些方法成本高且提供的指导有限。提出的NeuronFuzz框架通过利用内部安全神经元提供连续执行反馈，从而消除了模糊处理过程中对响应生成的需求，展现出其独特性。这种方法具有良好的动机，因为它允许更高效、更有效地评估模型安全性。NeuronFuzz构建了一个SafetyOracle，将安全神经元激活转化为连续的安全警报分数，从而能够识别安全敏感的模板位置并生成上下文兼容的变异。该方法在21个文本和多模态模型上进行了测试，在五个白盒源模型中实现了76-100%的越狱发现率，显著超越现有方法，提升幅度高达48个百分点，从而支持了其增强LLM安全评估的目标。
'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：'翻译中的隐秘'即未见的危害：使用'乌尔都语遗漏'分数测量跨脚本安全不一致性在LLM仇恨言论检测中的应用
乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都英语。在五个乌尔都脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）至31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%至9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the significant gap in safety evaluation for Urdu, a widely spoken language, in large language models (LLMs), highlighting its absence in mainstream assessments and the potential implications for content moderation reliability. Previous methods have largely overlooked Urdu, leading to inconsistencies in hate speech detection across different scripts, particularly between original Urdu and English translations. The proposed approach systematically evaluates five LLMs across various datasets, revealing substantial label instability and a notable 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, indicating harmful content that is misclassified when translated. This study contributes to the understanding of cross-script safety inconsistencies in LLMs and emphasizes the need for inclusive evaluation practices. The methodology involves testing LLMs on datasets that include Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, and English, uncovering performance discrepancies that support the necessity for improved safety measures in Urdu content moderation.
本研究关注乌尔都语在大型语言模型（LLMs）安全评估中的显著缺口，强调了内容审核可靠性可能受到的影响。以往的方法在很大程度上忽视了乌尔都语，导致安全评估中的不一致性，尤其是在原文和英文翻译之间。本研究提出对五个LLM在多个数据集上的系统评估，以测量跨脚本安全不一致性，结果显示“乌尔都语遗漏”率表明标签不稳定性令人担忧。该方法涉及对GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.5等模型在Nastaliq乌尔都语和罗马乌尔都语数据集上的测试，发现遗漏危害率在2.4%到9.9%之间。这些发现强调了对乌尔都语在LLM安全评估中进行专门研究的紧迫性，因为当前模型在不同脚本变体之间的安全保障存在显著差异。
ADVERSA: Measuring Multi-Turn Guardrail Degradation and Judge Reliability in Large Language Models
Authors: Harry Owiredu-Ashley
Venue: 2026 IEEE/ACIS 24th International Conference on Software Engineering Research, Management and Applications (SERA), Towson, MD, USA, 2026, pp. 414-417
First: 2026-03-10T03:00:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T05:47:14+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 12 figures. Independent research. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/Harry-Ashley/adversa-guardrail-degradation. Published in the 2026 IEEE/ACIS 24th International Conference on Software Engineering Research, Management and Applications (SERA), pp. 414-417. Recipient of the Best Paper Award at IEEE/ACIS SERA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Most adversarial evaluations of large language model (LLM) safety assess single prompts and report binary pass/fail outcomes, which fails to capture how safety properties evolve under sustained adversarial interaction. We present ADVERSA, an automated red-teaming framework that measures guardrail degradation dynamics as continuous per-round compliance trajectories rather than discrete jailbreak events. ADVERSA uses a fine-tuned 70B attacker model (ADVERSA-Red, Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct with QLoRA) that eliminates the attacker-side safety refusals that render off-the-shelf models unreliable as attackers, scoring victim responses on a structured 5-point rubric that treats partial compliance as a distinct measurable state. We report a controlled experiment across three frontier victim models (Claude Opus 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.2) using a triple-judge consensus architecture in which judge reliability is measured as a first-class research outcome rather than assumed. Across 15 conversations of up to 10 adversarial rounds, we observe a 26.7% jailbreak rate with an average jailbreak round of 1.25, suggesting that in this evaluation setting, successful jailbreaks were concentrated in early rounds rather than accumulating through sustained pressure. We document inter-judge agreement rates, self-judge scoring tendencies, attacker drift as a failure mode in fine-tuned attackers deployed out of their training distribution, and attacker refusals as a previously-underreported confound in victim resistance measurement. All limitations are stated explicitly. Attack prompts are withheld per responsible disclosure policy; all other experimental artifacts are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ADVERSA：测量大型语言模型中的多轮护栏退化和评审可靠性
大多数针对大型语言模型（LLM）安全性的对抗性评估仅评估单个提示并报告二元通过/失败结果，这未能捕捉安全属性在持续对抗交互下的演变。我们提出了ADVERSA，一个自动化红队框架，测量护栏退化动态，作为连续的每轮合规轨迹，而不是离散的越狱事件。ADVERSA使用经过微调的70B攻击者模型（ADVERSA-Red，Llama-3.1-70B-Instruct与QLoRA），消除了攻击者侧的安全拒绝，使现成模型在攻击者中不可靠，使用结构化的5分制评分受害者响应，将部分合规视为一个独特的可测量状态。我们报告了在三个前沿受害者模型（Claude Opus 4.6，Gemini 3.1 Pro，GPT-5.2）上进行的受控实验，使用三重评审共识架构，其中评审可靠性被视为一项一流的研究结果，而不是假设。在15次对话中，最多进行10轮对抗，我们观察到26.7%的越狱率，平均越狱轮次为1.25，表明在该评估环境中，成功的越狱集中在早期轮次，而不是通过持续压力积累。我们记录了评审间一致性率、自我评审评分倾向、在超出训练分布的微调攻击者中作为失败模式的攻击者漂移，以及作为受害者抵抗测量中先前未报告的混淆因素的攻击者拒绝。所有限制均明确说明。攻击提示根据负责任的披露政策被保留；所有其他实验材料均已发布。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing adversarial evaluations of large language models (LLMs), which typically assess safety through single prompts and binary outcomes, failing to capture the dynamics of safety properties during prolonged adversarial interactions. The proposed ADVERSA framework introduces a continuous measurement of guardrail degradation using a fine-tuned 70B attacker model, which improves reliability by eliminating safety refusals that affect off-the-shelf models. The contribution of this work lies in its innovative approach to evaluating judge reliability as a key outcome and documenting various failure modes in adversarial interactions. The methodology involves a controlled experiment with three advanced victim models and a triple-judge consensus architecture, revealing a 26.7% jailbreak rate across multiple conversations, indicating that successful jailbreaks tend to occur early rather than accumulate over time. These findings support the goal of providing a more nuanced understanding of LLM safety under sustained adversarial pressure.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLM）对抗性评估的局限性，这些评估通常通过单一提示和二元结果来评估安全性，未能捕捉到在持续对抗互动中安全属性的动态变化。提出的ADVERSA框架引入了一种连续测量护栏退化的方法，使用经过微调的70B攻击者模型，通过消除现成模型中常见的安全拒绝来提高可靠性。本文的贡献在于其创新的方法，评估评审者的可靠性并测量多个对抗回合中的合规轨迹，揭示了在对三种先进受害者模型进行的受控实验中，早期回合集中出现26.7%的越狱率。该方法不仅提供了对模型脆弱性的更细致理解，还确立了评审者可靠性作为关键研究成果，从而支持了增强LLM安全评估稳健性的目标。
NeuronGuard: Robust LLM Safety Alignment via Ablation-Aware Safety Signal Redistribution
Authors: Anjun Gao, Yueyang Quan, Yufei Xia, Zhuqing Liu, Minghong Fang
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-25T01:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-25T01:36:54+00:00
Comments: To appear in EMNLP 2026 (Findings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) remains brittle against a growing spectrum of attacks. Jailbreak attacks bypass safety mechanisms through crafted prompts, while neuron-level attacks directly prune safety-critical neurons post-deployment. Both exploit a common weakness: safety-relevant information concentrates in a sparse neuron subset. We present NeuronGuard, a fine-tuning-stage defense that simultaneously hardens LLMs against both attack classes by redistributing safety signals across a broader set of neurons. NeuronGuard dynamically identifies safety-critical neurons via periodically refreshed per-layer linear classifiers, forces refusal behavior under deliberate neuron ablation, and applies KL-divergence regularization for distributional consistency. A randomized gradient projection strategy preserves downstream task utility by resolving conflicts between the defense and task objectives. We provide a formal guarantee that NeuronGuard strictly reduces the attack success rate (ASR) upper bound, and experiments across three LLMs, six state-of-the-art attack strategies, and multimodal settings confirm near-zero ASR while maintaining task accuracy, including against white-box adaptive adversaries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronGuard：通过消融感知安全信号重分配实现稳健的LLM安全对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全对齐在面对日益增长的攻击谱时仍然脆弱。越狱攻击通过精心设计的提示绕过安全机制，而神经元级攻击则在部署后直接修剪安全关键神经元。两者都利用了一个共同的弱点：安全相关信息集中在稀疏的神经元子集中。我们提出了NeuronGuard，这是一种微调阶段的防御方法，通过在更广泛的神经元集上重新分配安全信号，来同时增强LLMs对这两类攻击的抵御能力。NeuronGuard通过定期更新的每层线性分类器动态识别安全关键神经元，在故意消融神经元时强制拒绝行为，并应用KL散度正则化以保持分布一致性。一种随机梯度投影策略通过解决防御与任务目标之间的冲突，保持下游任务的效用。我们提供了一个正式保证，NeuronGuard严格降低攻击成功率（ASR）上限，并且在三种LLM、六种最先进的攻击策略和多模态设置下的实验确认了接近零的ASR，同时保持任务准确性，包括对抗白盒自适应对手的情况。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of large language models (LLMs) to various safety attacks, particularly jailbreak and neuron-level attacks, which exploit the concentration of safety-relevant information in a limited set of neurons. Previous methods have struggled to provide robust defenses against these attacks, often failing to maintain task performance while enhancing safety. The proposed approach, NeuronGuard, differs by redistributing safety signals across a wider array of neurons, dynamically identifying safety-critical neurons, and employing techniques such as KL-divergence regularization and randomized gradient projection to balance safety and task objectives. This paper contributes a novel fine-tuning defense mechanism that guarantees a reduction in attack success rates while preserving task accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that NeuronGuard achieves near-zero attack success rates across multiple LLMs and attack strategies, effectively supporting its goal of enhancing safety alignment without compromising performance.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）在面对各种安全攻击时的脆弱性，特别是越狱攻击和神经元级攻击，这些攻击利用安全相关信息集中在有限数量的神经元中的弱点。以往的方法在提供对这些攻击的强大防御方面存在困难，因此需要改进安全对齐策略。所提出的方法NeuronGuard通过在更广泛的神经元中重新分配安全信号来增强安全性，采用动态识别安全关键神经元、在神经元消融下强制拒绝行为和KL散度正则化等技术。这种方法具有良好的动机，旨在在显著降低攻击成功率（ASR）的同时保持任务性能。实验结果表明，NeuronGuard在多个LLM和攻击策略中实现了接近零的ASR，同时保持了任务准确性，从而支持其预期目标。
Register Shifts Break LLM Safety: A Bengali Benchmark with Culturally Grounded Harms
Authors: Naymul Islam, Nusrat Jahan Lia, Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Sabik Bin Sultan, Abdullah Khan Zehady
First: 2026-08-23T10:09:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-23T10:09:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bengali is the seventh-most-spoken language globally, yet LLM safety evaluation remains overwhelmingly English-centric. We introduce BanglaSafe, a benchmark of 879 Bengali prompts combining 309 natively authored prompts with 570 expert-reviewed prompts, spanning 17 culturally grounded harm categories and five prompting conditions that vary language, writing style, and authority framing. Evaluating 18 frontier LLMs, we find that over half of all responses are unsafe or partially unsafe (53.6%) while 14.7% contains strictly harmful content, and that the strongest observed effect is not the switch from English to Bengali but the choice of writing style within Bengali: the same harmful request phrased as a formal newspaper investigation succeeds 17 percentage points more often than the same request phrased as a casual message, with no adversarial engineering involved. We further show that existing safety classifiers struggle to reliably evaluate Bengali content, with even frontier models failing on nearly half of all cases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注册班次突破LLM安全：一个具有文化基础危害的孟加拉基准
孟加拉语是全球第七大语言，但LLM安全评估仍然以英语为中心。我们推出了BanglaSafe，这是一个包含879个孟加拉语提示的基准，结合了309个本土创作的提示和570个专家审核的提示，涵盖17个文化基础的危害类别和五种变化语言、写作风格和权威框架的提示条件。评估18个前沿LLM时，我们发现超过一半的响应是不安全或部分不安全的（53.6%），而14.7%的内容包含严格有害的信息，观察到的最强效应不是从英语切换到孟加拉语，而是在孟加拉语中选择写作风格：同样的有害请求以正式的报纸调查形式表达时成功率比以随意消息形式表达时高出17个百分点，且没有涉及对抗性工程。我们进一步表明，现有的安全分类器在可靠评估孟加拉语内容方面存在困难，甚至前沿模型在近一半的案例中也未能成功。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the lack of safety evaluation for large language models (LLMs) in Bengali, despite it being the seventh-most-spoken language globally, highlighting the English-centric focus of existing methods. Previous approaches have not adequately assessed culturally specific harms, leading to significant gaps in understanding LLM safety in non-English contexts. The proposed method, BanglaSafe, introduces a benchmark of 879 Bengali prompts that include both natively authored and expert-reviewed content across various harm categories and prompting conditions, effectively addressing the limitations of prior evaluations. This paper contributes by revealing that over half of the responses from 18 evaluated LLMs are unsafe, with a notable finding that writing style in Bengali significantly influences the safety of responses. The methodology demonstrates that existing safety classifiers are inadequate for Bengali content, as they fail to evaluate nearly half of the cases accurately, underscoring the need for culturally grounded assessments in LLM safety.

Redteaming Leading Arabic LLMs with ASAS
Authors: Fidaa Abed, Haidar Khan, M Saiful Bari, Babar Khan, Abdalghani Abujabal
First: 2026-08-22T14:34:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-22T14:34:08+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figues, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As the adoption of large language models (LLMs) grows in Arabic-speaking regions, ensuring their safety and cultural alignment is increasingly critical. However, Arabic LLM safety remains underexplored, especially in adversarial evaluation settings. We introduce the Arabic Safety Index (ASAS), the first fully human-curated Arabic benchmark for redteaming LLMs. ASAS contains 801 prompts spanning 8 safety categories and 8 attack strategies, with ideal responses in Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). We conduct a redteaming evaluation across seven leading models with Arabic capabilities, including GPT-4o, Claude 3.7 Sonnet, and regional models such as ALLaM and FANAR. Human annotators rate responses using a structured 4-point safety scale, revealing that most models fail to defend against 50% of unsafe prompts. Our findings highlight major safety gaps in high-harm categories such as weapons and illicit substances, with direct and obfuscation-based attacks proving most effective. The results also show that language alignment does not readily transfer across languages, and that automated safety judges (e.g., GPT-4o) perform poorly compared to human annotators. ASAS provides a culturally grounded benchmark and redteaming protocol to drive progress in Arabic LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用ASAS对阿拉伯大型语言模型进行红队测试
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在阿拉伯语地区的应用不断增长，确保其安全性和文化一致性变得愈发重要。然而，阿拉伯LLM的安全性仍然未得到充分探索，特别是在对抗性评估环境中。我们引入了阿拉伯安全指数（ASAS），这是第一个完全由人类策划的阿拉伯红队测试基准。ASAS包含801个提示，涵盖8个安全类别和8种攻击策略，并提供现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）的理想响应。我们对七个具有阿拉伯语能力的领先模型进行了红队评估，包括GPT-4o、Claude 3.7 Sonnet以及区域模型如ALLaM和FANAR。人类注释者使用结构化的4分安全评分标准对响应进行评分，结果显示大多数模型未能抵御50%的不安全提示。我们的研究结果突显了在武器和非法物质等高危类别中的主要安全缺口，直接攻击和混淆攻击被证明最为有效。结果还表明，语言一致性并不能轻易跨语言转移，自动安全评估者（例如GPT-4o）的表现远不及人类注释者。ASAS提供了一个文化基础的基准和红队测试协议，以推动阿拉伯LLM安全性的进展。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for safety and cultural alignment in large language models (LLMs) used in Arabic-speaking regions, an area that has been largely neglected in adversarial evaluations. Previous methods have not adequately explored Arabic LLM safety, leading to significant gaps in understanding their vulnerabilities. The proposed approach introduces the Arabic Safety Index (ASAS), a comprehensive human-curated benchmark designed specifically for redteaming Arabic LLMs, which includes 801 prompts across various safety categories and attack strategies. This method effectively highlights the shortcomings of existing models, revealing that many fail to respond safely to 50% of unsafe prompts, particularly in high-harm areas. The evaluation of seven leading Arabic LLMs demonstrates that human annotators provide more reliable safety assessments than automated systems, underscoring the need for culturally relevant benchmarks to enhance LLM safety in Arabic contexts.
本研究关注阿拉伯大型语言模型（LLMs）在安全性和文化一致性方面的关键需求，这一领域在对抗性评估背景下仍然被严重忽视。以往的方法缺乏全面的阿拉伯LLM安全性评估基准，导致对其脆弱性的理解存在重大缺口。提出的阿拉伯安全指数（ASAS）引入了一个包含801个跨多个安全类别和攻击策略的人为策划基准，有效解决了现有评估的不足。本文的贡献在于提供了一个结构化的红队协议，并揭示许多领先的阿拉伯LLM无法处理50%的不安全提示，尤其是在高危领域。该方法涉及人类评估者对模型响应进行4分制安全评分，表明自动安全评估不如人工评估有效，从而支持ASAS在提升阿拉伯LLM安全性方面的必要性。
CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Chengxiao Wang, Enyi Jiang, Xiaojing Liao, Sanmi Koyejo
First: 2026-08-21T16:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T16:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLEAR：用于保持效用的LLM安全对齐的连续潜在适配器路由
提高大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全性往往会以效用为代价，因为全局应用的安全调优可能会影响模型对有害和良性输入的响应。我们提出了\textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing（CLEAR），这是一种条件安全适应框架，使用轻量级的隐藏状态门控来持续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度。CLEAR旨在减少有害的完成，同时避免对冻结主干的不必要更改，这可能会降低对良性提示的性能。对广泛使用的安全和效用基准的实验表明，CLEAR在HarmBench上提高了鲁棒性，同时减少了全局应用安全调优（如SFT或标准低秩适配（LoRA））所观察到的效用下降。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench ASR从32.3\%降低到0.5\%，同时保留了大部分基础模型的效用，并在GSM8K准确率上比全局应用的SFT或LoRA高出多达7.1个百分点。这些结果表明，CLEAR是改善LLM对齐中安全与效用权衡的有希望的机制。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) without compromising their utility, as traditional safety tuning methods often lead to a decline in model performance on benign inputs. Previous approaches, such as globally applied safety tuning and standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA), have been found to degrade utility while attempting to enhance safety. The proposed method, Continuous Latent Adapter Routing (CLEAR), introduces a conditional safety adaptation framework that utilizes a lightweight hidden-state gate to modulate the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter, thereby minimizing harmful outputs while preserving the model's performance on benign prompts. This approach is well-motivated as it seeks to balance safety and utility effectively. The methodology involves rigorous testing on safety and utility benchmarks, revealing that CLEAR significantly reduces harmful completions on HarmBench and enhances performance on tasks like GSM8K, achieving a reduction in HarmBench ASR from 32.3% to 0.5% and improving GSM8K accuracy by up to 7.1 percentage points compared to existing methods, thus supporting its goal of optimizing the safety-utility trade-off in LLM alignment.
本研究解决了在不影响大型语言模型（LLMs）效用的情况下提高其安全性的问题，因为传统的安全调优方法往往会降低模型在良性输入上的性能。以往的方法，如全局应用的安全调优和标准低秩适配（LoRA），在增强安全性的同时，已被证明会对效用产生负面影响。所提出的方法，连续潜在适配器路由（CLEAR），引入了一种轻量级的隐藏状态门控，允许对安全低秩适配器的激活强度进行持续控制，从而在最小化有害输出的同时保持模型在良性提示上的性能。本文贡献了一种新颖的框架，有效平衡了LLM对齐中的安全性和效用。通过在安全性和效用基准上的实验，CLEAR显示出显著的改进，将Llama-3-8B-Instruct上的HarmBench ASR从32.3%降低到0.5%，同时在GSM8K上比传统方法提高了多达7.1个百分点的准确率，表明其在解决安全性与效用权衡方面的有效性。
RouteScan: A Non-Intrusive Approach to Auditing MoE LLMs Safety via Expert Routing Telemetry
Authors: Bo Lv, Zhiheng Xu, KeDong Xiu, Ruyi Ding, Tianhang Zheng, Zhibo Wang, Kui Ren
First: 2026-05-24T02:06:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T12:55:57+00:00
Comments: 11 pages. Revised manuscript with expanded experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), safety auditing becomes necessary to verify whether these models produce or facilitate harmful behaviors during operation. However, existing content-based auditing methods typically require access to user prompts, model internals, or outputs, potentially exposing sensitive user information and creating a tension between LLM safety and user privacy. On the other hand, we observe that, in MoE models, different inputs induce different sparse expert-routing patterns, which produce measurable footprints in low-level GPU execution telemetry. We refer to these hardware-observable signals induced by expert-routing decisions as expert routing telemetry; they are derived from GPU execution rather than from router logits or token-level routing assignments. Inspired by this observation, we propose RouteScan, a non-intrusive auditing framework for detecting harmful behaviors through such routing-induced GPU telemetry. Specifically, RouteScan utilizes the number of active GPU threads allocated to expert modules during the prefilling phase as a discriminative micro-architectural fingerprint, and builds a lightweight detection pipeline that isolates cross-domain invariant risk indicators for the precise identification of malicious prompts. Comprehensive evaluations on four open-source MoE LLMs with distinct routing designs demonstrate that RouteScan achieves strong generalization, with an AUROC exceeding 0.91 on unseen harmful domains. Moreover, privacy stress tests show that, although aggregated execution telemetry retains input-related attribute information, full prompts and exact sensitive fields cannot be reliably recovered under the evaluated attacks.
中文标题/摘要

随着混合专家（MoE）架构在大规模语言模型（LLMs）中的日益采用，安全审计变得必要，以验证这些模型在操作过程中是否产生或促进有害行为。然而，现有的基于内容的审计方法通常需要访问用户提示、模型内部或输出，可能会暴露敏感用户信息，并在LLM安全性与用户隐私之间产生紧张关系。另一方面，我们观察到，在MoE模型中，不同的输入会引发不同的稀疏专家路由模式，这在低级GPU执行遥测中产生可测量的足迹。我们将这些由专家路由决策引发的硬件可观察信号称为专家路由遥测；它们源自GPU执行，而不是来自路由器logits或令牌级路由分配。受到这一观察的启发，我们提出了RouteScan，一种通过这种路由引发的GPU遥测检测有害行为的非侵入式审计框架。具体而言，RouteScan利用在预填充阶段分配给专家模块的活跃GPU线程数量作为区分性微架构指纹，并构建一个轻量级检测管道，隔离跨域不变风险指标，以精确识别恶意提示。对四个具有不同路由设计的开源MoE LLMs的全面评估表明，RouteScan实现了强大的泛化能力，在未见的有害领域中AUROC超过0.91。此外，隐私压力测试表明，尽管聚合的执行遥测保留了与输入相关的属性信息，但在评估的攻击下，无法可靠地恢复完整提示和确切的敏感字段。

Certified Multi-Turn Robustness for LLM Safety via Compositional Bounds and Safety Persistence
Authors: Yang Liu, Bin Chong, Wenkai Yang, Shuai Zhang, Yancheng Chen, Feiyu Han, GuoZhen, Cheng Zhang, Huaibing Xie, Changze Lv, Shihan Dou, Pluto Zhou
First: 2026-08-21T07:43:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T07:43:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to multi-turn jailbreak attacks that progressively manipulate conversation context. Existing certified robustness methods are limited to single-turn inputs; naive multi-turn composition yields bounds that degrade exponentially in the number of turns. We introduce Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR), a framework that models conversational safety via State-Adversarial MDPs and defines $k$-turn certified robustness as the worst-case safety probability across $k$ adversarial turns. MTCR comprises: (i) compositional certification via embedding-space mode decomposition, yielding tighter certified lower bounds than naive multiplication; (ii) $(α,β)$-safety persistence, improving the degradation rate from $\underline{p}^{k}$ to $β^k$ (with $β> \underline{p}$) and yielding interpretable horizon estimates; (iii) matching information-theoretic upper bounds establishing tightness; and (iv) a unified algorithm combining these results. Experiments on six LLMs under $ε$-bounded and Crescendo-style attacks confirm that empirical safety consistently exceeds the certified bounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过组合界限和安全持久性实现LLM安全的认证多轮鲁棒性
大型语言模型（LLMs）易受多轮越狱攻击，这些攻击逐步操控对话上下文。现有的认证鲁棒性方法仅限于单轮输入；简单的多轮组合导致界限在轮数上呈指数下降。我们引入了多轮认证鲁棒性（MTCR），这是一个通过状态对抗MDP建模对话安全的框架，并将$k$轮认证鲁棒性定义为$k$轮对抗中的最坏情况安全概率。MTCR包括：（i）通过嵌入空间模式分解进行组合认证，产生比简单乘法更紧的认证下界；（ii）$(α,β)$-安全持久性，将降级率从$\underline{p}^{k}$改善到$β^k$（其中$β> \underline{p}$），并产生可解释的时间估计；（iii）匹配信息论上界限以建立紧密性；（iv）结合这些结果的统一算法。在$ε$-有界和Crescendo风格攻击下对六个LLM的实验确认，经验安全性始终超过认证界限。

Previous methods for certified robustness were limited to single-turn inputs, and naive approaches to multi-turn composition resulted in exponentially degrading bounds. The proposed Multi-Turn Certified Robustness (MTCR) framework improves upon existing methods by modeling conversational safety through State-Adversarial MDPs and defining certified robustness across multiple turns, thus providing tighter bounds and better performance. Calibrate-Then-Delegate: Safety Monitoring with Risk and Budget Guarantees via Model Cascades
Authors: Edoardo Pona, Milad Kazemi, Mehran Hosseini, Yali Du, David Watson, Osvaldo Simeone, Nicola Paoletti
First: 2026-04-15T11:05:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-21T07:17:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monitoring LLM safety at scale requires balancing cost and accuracy: a cheap latent-space probe can screen every input, but hard cases should be escalated to a more expensive expert. Existing cascades delegate based on probe uncertainty, but uncertainty is a poor proxy for the utility of an expert call, as it ignores whether the expert would actually improve the prediction. To address this problem, we introduce Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD), a model-cascade approach that provides probabilistic guarantees on either computation cost or safety performance while enabling instance-level (streaming) decisions. CTD builds on a novel delegation value (DV) probe, a lightweight model operating on the same internal representations as the safety probe that predicts the benefit of replacing the probe score with the expert score after an expert call. CTD calibrates a threshold on the DV signal using held-out data and multiple hypothesis testing, yielding finite-sample guarantees while optimising the complementary objective. Evaluated on four safety datasets using learned-attention probes, CTD improves over uncertainty-based delegation over much of the budget range, avoids unnecessary expert calls, and extracts positive-value subsets even from groups where the expert score is worse than the probe score on average. Ablations across four probe architectures show that these findings are not specific to any particular architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：校准后委派：通过模型级联实现风险和预算保证的安全监测
大规模监测LLM安全性需要平衡成本和准确性：便宜的潜在空间探测器可以筛选每个输入，但困难案例应升级到更昂贵的专家。现有的级联基于探测器的不确定性进行委派，但不确定性并不是专家调用效用的良好代理，因为它忽略了专家是否真的会改善预测。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了校准后委派（CTD），一种模型级联方法，提供计算成本或安全性能的概率保证，同时支持实例级（流式）决策。CTD基于一种新颖的委派价值（DV）探测器，该轻量级模型在与安全探测器相同的内部表示上运行，预测在专家调用后用专家分数替代探测器分数的好处。CTD使用保留数据和多重假设检验对DV信号进行阈值校准，提供有限样本保证，同时优化互补目标。在四个安全数据集上使用学习注意力探测器进行评估，CTD在大部分预算范围内优于基于不确定性的委派，避免了不必要的专家调用，并从专家分数平均低于探测器分数的组中提取出正值子集。对四种探测器架构的消融实验表明，这些发现并不特定于任何特定架构。

The research addresses the challenge of monitoring the safety of large language models (LLMs) at scale, focusing on the need to balance cost and accuracy in safety assessments. The proposed Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD) method introduces a novel delegation value probe that evaluates the potential benefit of expert intervention, allowing for instance-level decision-making while providing probabilistic guarantees on computation costs or safety performance. The paper contributes to the field by demonstrating that CTD outperforms uncertainty-based delegation across various budget scenarios, minimizes unnecessary expert calls, and successfully identifies valuable subsets even when expert scores are lower than probe scores on average, validated through experiments on four safety datasets. aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy
Authors: Fatih Deniz, Yazan Boshmaf, Dorde Popovic, Issa Khalil
First: 2026-08-20T20:33:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-20T20:33:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：aiXamine：统一黑箱评估大型语言模型安全性、保密性和隐私中的跨维权衡
已部署的大型语言模型（LLMs）中的关键失效模式是跨维的：一个模型在安全对齐上得分99.3，但拒绝三分之一的良性查询，或者在每个能力指标上都有所提升，但隐私损失21分。现有的评估框架独立评估安全性、保密性和隐私，无法检测这些模式。我们引入了aiXamine，一个统一的黑箱平台，评估LLM在安全性、保密性和隐私方面的可信度，作为相互依赖的属性。aiXamine通过自动化红队管道协调46个测试，涵盖九项服务，生成从提示级诊断到跨服务权衡分析的分层风险概况，使在相同条件下对专有和开放权重系统的可重复比较成为可能。通过超过5000次测试运行将aiXamine应用于120多个LLM，我们进行了迄今为止最大的联合安全性、保密性和隐私研究，揭示了三种在单轴评估中不可见的跨维现象。首先，安全执行会产生可量化的安全税：更强的对齐系统性地增加过度拒绝，迫使提供者在保护和效用之间做出选择。其次，隐私与其他可信度维度几乎正交，标准对齐无法捕捉。第三，我们识别并正式表征了蒸馏引起的鲁棒性崩溃：没有政策校正的离政策蒸馏导致熵崩溃，灾难性地破坏鲁棒性（56.9$\to$2.6）在相同的基础架构上。这些发现，加上规模的递减收益和类别依赖的安全行为，表明可信度本质上是多维的：在一个轴上的进展并不保证，并且可能会积极破坏其他轴上的进展，而当前的对齐方法将其视为单一目标。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical failure modes in large language models (LLMs), which often exhibit cross-dimensional trade-offs in safety, security, and privacy that existing evaluation frameworks fail to detect. Previous methods assessed these dimensions independently, leading to incomplete evaluations and an inability to identify interdependencies. The proposed approach, aiXamine, is a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across these dimensions as interdependent properties, employing an automated red-teaming pipeline to conduct 46 tests across nine services. This methodology allows for the generation of hierarchical risk profiles and facilitates reproducible comparisons of various systems. The application of aiXamine to over 120 LLMs revealed three significant phenomena: a safety tax associated with stronger alignment, the near-orthogonality of privacy to other dimensions, and the identification of distillation-induced robustness collapse, highlighting the multi-dimensional nature of trustworthiness in LLMs and the inadequacy of current alignment methods.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）中的关键失效模式，强调现有评估框架独立评估安全性、安全性和隐私性，未能捕捉它们之间的相互依赖关系。提出的方法aiXamine与过去的方法不同，它提供了一个统一的黑箱评估平台，进行46项测试，涵盖九项服务，从而允许对LLM可信度进行全面分析。这种方法的动机充分，因为它揭示了传统评估中被忽视的安全性、安全性和隐私性之间的复杂相互作用。本文的贡献在于识别出三个重要的跨维度现象，包括安全性与效用之间的权衡、隐私与其他维度的独立性，以及蒸馏对鲁棒性的有害影响。通过将aiXamine应用于超过120个LLMs，并进行超过5000次测试，该研究实现了迄今为止最大的联合安全性、安全性和隐私性评估，表明在一个领域的改善可能导致其他领域的下降，从而强调了在可信度评估中需要多维度视角的重要性。
Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics
Authors: Mohamed Akrout, Olivera Kotevska, Dan Wilson
First: 2026-08-20T02:50:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-20T02:50:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基于DMD的提示-响应嵌入动态分类来强化LLM安全性
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地应用于高风险场景，但它们生成有毒、有害或违反政策内容的倾向带来了重大风险。在黑箱方式下高效检测这些不安全输出仍然是一个开放挑战。本文扩展了最近提出的用于幻觉检测的动态系统框架，以进行LLM安全分类。通过将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，并为安全和不安全状态拟合独立的基于Koopman的预测模型，我们使用新的微分残差分数对新输出进行分类，该分数比较安全和不安全状态的预测误差。一个关键贡献是结合了提示和响应的嵌入动态，得到了捕捉关键交互模式的拟合Koopman算子。我们在三个安全基准上使用三种嵌入模型评估了我们的黑箱方法。结果表明，结合提示嵌入可以带来一致的改进，特别是在与因果解码器（例如Llama-3）配对时，针对交互依赖的违规行为，而仅响应的违规行为则更受益于密集语义嵌入表示。这些发现为使用动态系统分析AI系统打开了大门，而不是使用AI建模动态系统的主流范式。

Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets
Authors: Zhida He, Xiaoyu Wen, Han Qi, Ziyuan Zhou, Peng Yu, Jiajia Li, Chaochao Lu, Qiaosheng Zhang
First: 2026-08-18T04:26:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-18T04:26:39+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 8 figures, 13 tables. Code: https://github.com/xsddys/Fair-ASR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.
中文标题/摘要
标题：公平ASR：在共享目标调用预算下重新评估黑箱越狱
可靠的越狱评估对于评估大型语言模型（LLM）的安全性至关重要，但现有大多数研究仅依赖攻击成功率（ASR），而未考虑其对攻击预算的依赖，导致方法之间的不公平比较。现有的计算感知评估将异构资源简化为FLOPs，这对于黑箱模型难以估计，并未捕捉资源特定的约束。为了提供可比较的评估基础，我们引入了公平ASR，这是一种在共享目标调用预算B下评估黑箱越狱攻击的评估协议，使用目标调用作为直接可观察和方法无关的比较轴，同时单独跟踪攻击者调用以进行效率分析。我们在公平ASR协议下重新评估了11个代表性攻击，发现攻击排名在目标调用预算之间发生了显著变化，简单的随机扰动和手工制作的模板在平等目标访问下仍然具有高度竞争力，并且没有评估的LLM驱动方法在目标和攻击者调用中都有效率。受到这一效率差距的启发，我们引入了ReCode，这是一种组合预算高效的攻击，结合了去敏感重写和公平ASR识别的两种有效低成本原语。在20个目标调用的预算下，ReCode在GPT-5上实现了85%的ASR，同时平均每个请求仅需7.19个攻击者调用，显示出在目标和攻击者调用中的强效率。

Previous evaluations often reduce resource usage to FLOPs, which are challenging to estimate for black-box models and overlook specific resource constraints. To address the identified efficiency gap, the paper introduces ReCode, a budget-efficient attack that integrates desensitization rewriting with effective low-cost primitives, achieving an 85% ASR on GPT-5 with only 7.19 attacker calls per request under a budget of 20 target calls, thus supporting the goal of improved efficiency in jailbreak evaluations.
